(ii) Inca political entropy $H_{\mathrm{inca}}$ is stable through the Cajamarca capture (1532~CE), then collapses to zero by 1537~CE; the dominant break is at Vilcabamba ($F=120.7$), not Cajamarca ($F=11.5$). (iii) Four of six layers reach the critical regime at 1532~CE, while infrastructure stays marginally below threshold ($\mathrm{ICT}=0.495$); trade and political reach the highest values ($\mathrm{ICT}=0.976$, $0.968$), marking Cajamarca as a synchronised multi-layer phase transition. (iv) Spanish legal administration converges to the infrastructure topology by 1572~CE ($W_1=5\times10^{-6}\approx0$): Substitution Topology, infrastructure-preserving collapse via topology transfer. (v) Legal--effective divergence decays from $W_1=0.591$ (1532) to $0.002$ (1572), quantifying resolution of colonial legal fiction.
  Together, these results support a distinct collapse mode---Substitution Topology---differing from erosion-dominated Roman/Byzantine collapse, Han fragmentation, and Aztec military destruction.

</details>

*Alexei Vazquez*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类在时间竞争下如何决定参与共同活动而非独自行动，尤其是在存在合作风险的情况下，个体如何战略性地评估共享任务的价值。

Method: 采用优先级队列模型，将共享任务的优先级设为由代理自主选择，其价值经合作者不参与的风险折现；通过理论推导获得鞍结分岔的闭式解，并扩展至网络情形，分析度分布与群体活动的最大规模。

Result: 模型出现从可持续联合活动的耦合相到吸收性孤立相的不连续相变；相变仅在一个狭窄参数窗口内保留重尾事件间隔统计，且指数由耦合时间比例决定；在网络中，注意力分配导致临界度存在，超过则无法维持耦合状态，且群体规模存在上限。

Conclusion: 引入战略选择后，传统队列模型的无标度特性不再成立；人类合作的持续性受记忆时间、网络连接度和群体大小限制，且系统崩溃不可逆，除非单方面持续投入；结果解释了通信记录中的活跃关系容量与中断后关系衰退现象。

Abstract: What decides whether people do things together or separately? Many activities cannot be carried out alone, and an individual must rank them against the private tasks competing for the same time. We address this within the priority-queue description of human activity by letting each agent choose the priority of a shared task rather than drawing it from a fixed distribution: the value of the joint activity, discounted by the estimated risk that the partner will not take part. Participation becomes strategic, and the model acquires a phase transition. A coupled phase, in which joint activity is sustained, is separated from an absorbing solitary phase by a saddle-node bifurcation that we obtain in closed form. The transition is discontinuous and the solitary phase is absorbing, so collapse is irreversible unless an agent persists unilaterally for of order one memory time, a cost we also compute. The heavy-tailed interevent statistics that motivate queueing models of human dynamics survive only in a narrow window at the transition, and there the exponent is fixed by the fraction of time spent coupled rather than by queue length: the universality classes of the non-strategic model do not survive the introduction of choice. On a network, attention divides as $1/(k+a)$ and fixes a critical degree beyond which no coupled state exists, so the solitary phase percolates according to the Molloy--Reed criterion with the second moment truncated at that degree --- formally an attack on hubs, with no attacker. For group activities the critical degree falls as the $(m-1)$th root, implying a maximum group size. The transition organises two quantities already measured in communication records: a finite capacity for keeping ties active, and the decay of ties whose rhythm is interrupted.

</details>

### [94] [Refined outer-approximation algorithms for monotonic optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13694)
*Ahmed Rashwan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出三种算法改进以解决单调优化中polyblock外逼近算法（POA）可扩展性差的问题，显著提升了计算效率，并开发了开源Python库polyblocks。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POA算法在处理大规模问题时因顶点数量指数增长而导致内存和计算开销过大，现有方法难以应对复杂实例。

Method: 提出了广义锚点选择、松弛最优条件和向量化多节点处理三种改进，并采用树形结构实现高效紧凑的外逼近表示。

Result: 实验表明新方法比标准POA快一个数量级以上，能求解现有变体失败的问题。

Conclusion: 所提算法显著提升POA的可扩展性和实用性，配套开源工具支持后续研究。

Abstract: Monotonic optimisation is a broad class of non-convex problems formulated in terms of monotone functions. Such problems are commonly solved via the polyblock outer-approximation algorithm (POA), a branch-and-bound method that iteratively refines a rectangular outer-approximation of the feasible set. POA scales poorly, however: the number of vertices needed to describe the approximation can grow exponentially, leading to large memory requirements and increasingly expensive subroutines. To address these limitations, we propose three algorithmic improvements: a generalised anchor selection that yields an optimal balanced monotonicity cut, a relaxed optimality condition that guarantees finite termination without continuity assumptions, and a vectorised variant that processes multiple nodes concurrently. We further develop an efficient tree-based implementation, which accelerates POA's core subroutines while storing the outer-approximation compactly. Numerical experiments show that these improvements yield order-of-magnitude speed-ups over standard POA and solve problems on which existing variants fail. Finally, we introduce polyblocks, an open-source Python package implementing the proposed algorithms alongside a framework for developing new POA variants, available at https://github.com/RashwanA/polyblocks.

</details>


### [95] [Multi-period Value-at-Risk Constrained Portfolio Optimization via DC Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13748)
*Thi Thu Van Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有有限场景风险价值（VaR）约束、交易成本和多样化正则化的多期投资组合优化问题，提出了一种结合惯性外推与增强线搜索的投影惯性增强型DC算法（iBDCA），并证明了其收敛性。数值实验表明该方法在风险控制和收益权衡方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的投资组合优化方法在处理VaR约束和交易成本时存在非凸非光滑难题，且缺乏有效算法保证收敛性和实际表现。本文旨在通过差值凸优化框架设计高效算法以解决此类问题。

Method: 利用有限场景下的VaR-CVaR恒等式，构建惩罚型差值凸（DC）规划模型，并提出一种融合惯性外推、目标函数保护机制和增强线搜索的投影惯性增强型DC算法（iBDCA）。理论分析证明该算法生成单调递减序列且具有良好的收敛性质。

Result: 所提iBDCA算法在相同问题实例上优于标准DCA和BDCA；回测结果显示其在实现收益、风险控制、交易成本和经验VaR之间具有良好权衡。算法在满足局部无平局条件下具备整体序列收敛性和局部R-线性收敛速率。

Conclusion: iBDCA是一种有效求解带VaR约束的非凸非光滑投资组合优化问题的算法，兼具理论保证和实证优势，适用于复杂现实投资环境。

Abstract: We study a multi-period portfolio optimization problem with finite-scenario Value-at-Risk (VaR) constraints, transaction costs, and diversification regularization. Using a finite-scenario VaR--CVaR identity, we derive a penalized difference-of-convex (DC) formulation over the underlying convex portfolio set. To solve the resulting nonsmooth and nonconvex problem, we propose a projected inertial Boosted Difference-of-Convex Functions Algorithm (iBDCA) that combines inertial extrapolation, an objective safeguard, and a boosted line search. We prove that the method is well defined, generates a monotonically decreasing objective sequence, and has only critical accumulation points for the penalized DC problem. Under a local no-ties condition at an accumulation point, we further establish whole sequence convergence with a local \(R\)-linear rate. Numerical experiments compare iBDCA with DCA and standard BDCA on matched problem instances. Out-of-sample backtests additionally include equal-weight and buy-and-hold benchmarks to illustrate the trade-offs among realized return, risk, transaction costs, and empirical VaR control.

</details>


### [96] [Best Response Dynamics for Zero-Sum Dynamic Games with Partial-Asymmetric Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13755)
*Yuxiang Guan,Iman Shames,Tyler H. Summers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了在部分和非对称信息下的零和随机线性二次动态博弈，提出基于最佳响应动态的方法来应对信念表示和心智理论挑战，并通过数值实验发现博弈值在几次迭代后收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非对称信息下动态博弈中信念表示与心智理论带来的挑战，克服传统动态规划方法难以分解的问题。

Method: 采用最佳响应动态方法，推导出纯线性动态输出反馈控制策略类中每个玩家的最佳响应表达式，并分析高阶信念状态的影响。

Result: 得到了玩家最佳响应的显式表达式，发现随着迭代产生高阶信念状态导致内部状态无限维，但博弈值在少数几次迭代后即收敛。

Conclusion: 尽管非对称信息导致信念复杂化，但高阶信念对博弈结果影响有限，低阶近似即可有效逼近博弈值。

Abstract: This work studies a class of zero-sum stochastic linear quadratic dynamic games (LQDGs) under partial and asymmetric information. Information asymmetry introduces fundamental challenges related to \textit{belief representation} and \textit{theory of mind}, where players must impute belief states and estimates of other players to inform their strategies. Existing work highlights the difficulty of applying dynamic programming-like decomposition approach to these problems. An alternative approach based on \textit{best response dynamics} is proposed, which provides insights into belief representation and theory of mind challenges. Explicit expressions for each player's best response within the class of pure linear dynamic output feedback control strategies are derived, where the internal state dimension of each control is an integer multiple of the system state dimension. As players iteratively update their best responses, they form increasingly higher-order belief states, leading to infinite-dimensional internal states. However, numerical results reveal that the game's value converges after only a few iterations, suggesting that higher-order belief states provide vanishing benefit. This work further conducts numerical experiments to analyze the impact of asymmetric beliefs, belief orders, relative controllability and observability, and direct feed-through on a linear quadratic pursuit-evasion game's value.

</details>


### [97] [Recursive Filtering and Stochastic Control under Finite Partition-Based Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13825)
*Saul Díaz-Infante Velasco,Yofre H. Garcia,Jesús Adolfo Minjárez-Sosa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对部分可观测随机系统的滤波与最优控制框架，适用于多种区域信息机制，并通过有限维近似处理无限维信息空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为处理具有有限可测划分观测的隐状态空间的部分可观测系统，建立统一且灵活的滤波与控制框架。

Method: 基于测度层面构建类限制的非归一化条件测度，引入包含观测类及其条件测度的信息状态，将原问题转化为完全可观测的马尔可夫决策过程，并提出类依赖的有限维近似方法。

Result: 导出了贝尔曼方程，证明了平稳最优策略的存在性，给出了滤波误差与值函数误差之间的抽象误差界，并通过直方图近似示例验证了方法构造。

Conclusion: 该框架统一处理多种不完整观测机制，通过信息状态转换和有限维近似，实现了对复杂部分可观测系统的有效最优控制。

Abstract: We develop a filtering and optimal-control framework for partially observable stochastic systems in which each observation identifies a class of a finite measurable partition of the hidden state space. This structure covers regional-information mechanisms associated with threshold, quantized, censored, event-triggered, and intermittent observations, and allows observable classes with atomic, continuous, or mixed components. The formulation is constructed first at the level of measures: for each observable class, we define a class-restricted unnormalized conditional measure, and the posterior distribution is obtained by normalizing with its predictive probability. Based on this recursion, we introduce an information state consisting of the observed class and the conditional measure supported on it, thereby transforming the original problem into a fully observable Markov decision process. We formulate the discounted-cost criterion, derive the Bellman equation, and establish conditions for the existence of stationary optimal policies. To address the infinite-dimensional nature of the information space, we propose class-dependent finite-dimensional approximations capable of preserving both continuous components and atomic masses. We also derive an abstract bound linking the error of the approximate filter to the error of the value function. A reference model illustrates the construction through histogram-based approximations

</details>


### [98] [Nondegeneracy Conditions for Control Problems with Nonregular Mixed Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13834)
*Jorge Becerril,Karla Cortez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有单个非正则混合不等式约束的最优控制问题中的非退化性和正规性条件，指出退化现象是内在且不可避免的，并提出了四个可验证条件以确保非退化的乘子、纯电荷消失、正规性及电荷质量的显式边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对在单一时刻非正则的最优控制问题，现有方法常导致退化现象，无法获得有效的必要条件，因此需要建立新的非退化与正规性条件。

Method: 通过证明模型问题中退化的内在性，分析自然正则逼近的归一化乘子在弱*拓扑下无极限的性质，并引入Banach极限意义下的广义极限来处理纯电荷；提出四个可验证条件并结合终端时刻的平衡恒等式进行分析。

Result: 发现所有容许过程均通过纯电荷退化地满足必要条件；提出了四个可验证条件，分别保证非退化、纯电荷消失、正规性及电荷质量的上界；在非正则终端时刻，电荷质量与代价乘子之间存在决定正常性与否的平衡恒等式。

Conclusion: 退化现象在所研究的问题中是不可避免的，但通过提出的四个条件可实现非退化和正规性，从而为非正则最优控制问题提供了理论基础和分析工具。

Abstract: We establish nondegeneracy and normality conditions for optimal control problems with a single nonregular mixed inequality constraint, where purely finitely additive set functions (charges) appear as multipliers. We first prove for a model problem nonregular at a single instant that degeneration is intrinsic and unavoidable: every admissible process satisfies the necessary conditions degenerately via a pure charge, and normalized multipliers of natural regular approximations admit no weak$^*$ limit in $L^\infty([0,1];\mathbb{R})^*$, yielding only pure charges under every generalized limit, in the sense of Banach limits. Motivated by this analysis, we propose four verifiable conditions that progressively guarantee nondegenerate multipliers, the vanishing of the pure charge, normality, and explicit bounds on charge mass. Furthermore, we show that at a nonregular terminal instant, a balance identity ties the charge mass to the cost multiplier, deciding between complete abnormality and normality. We provide examples confirming the realizability and discussing the limits of each condition.

</details>


### [99] [First-Order Optimization as Minimum-Time Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13915)
*Liraz Mudrik,Isaac Kaminer,Pramod P. Khargonekar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将一阶优化问题建模为最短时间控制问题，通过分析状态可达性与曲率资源，为每个优化实例提供衡量复杂性的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 试图从控制论角度理解一阶优化的内在复杂性，而非仅关注最坏情况下的收敛界。

Method: 将优化迭代视为状态，梯度更新视为控制，目标集为梯度范数小于给定阈值的点；利用离散Pontryagin极值条件推导共轭梯度法，并引入Hessian生成的可达空间分析非二次情形下的可达性。

Result: 在强凸二次函数上重现共轭梯度法，其迭代满足离散Pontryagin条件；提出可控性指标作为查询次数的度量；在非二次情形下，曲率成为可验证的资源，某些实例可比其二次模型所需步数更少，且差距随维度无界增长。

Conclusion: 所提框架为每个具体优化实例提供了可计算的价值函数，作为衡量一阶方法性能的内在基准。

Abstract: We formulate first-order optimization as a minimum-time control problem. The iterate is the state, the update, a combination of the gradients observed so far, is the control, and the points with gradient norm at most a prescribed tolerance form the target set. For a fixed objective and start, the minimum number of oracle queries needed to reach the target is a value function: it measures the complexity of the instance, not the worst case over a class. On a strongly convex quadratic, the conjugate gradient iterates emerge from the discrete Pontryagin conditions, and the value is a controllability index. Beyond the quadratic, a Hessian-generated reachable span replaces the controllability matrix, and feasibility becomes a reachability question: whether a critical point can be reached at all, and in how many steps, is read from the span. Curvature is a resource: a checkable condition certifies that an instance needs fewer steps than the controllability index of its quadratic model at the minimizer, and the gap can grow without bound with the dimension. The value is thus a benchmark for the intrinsic difficulty of each instance, against which any first-order method can be measured.

</details>


### [100] [Benders Decomposition with Partial Non-Anticipativity Relaxation for Multi-Stage Stochastic Clean Energy Transition Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14105)
*Ahmet Emir Şener,Burak Kocuk,Tuğçe Yüksel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了校园级电热综合系统在战略和运行层面不确定性下的清洁能源转型规划，提出了一种多阶段随机混合整数规划模型，并结合鲁棒优化与改进的Benders分解算法进行求解，显著提升了计算效率与方案可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校园级能源系统清洁转型面临投资与运行多重不确定性，传统方法难以兼顾长期决策与短期波动，需发展更高效可靠的优化框架。

Method: 构建多阶段随机混合整数规划模型，结合箱型不确定集的鲁棒重构，并设计带有部分非预期性松弛的Benders分解算法，辅以有效不等式和两阶段割平面策略提升求解效率。

Result: 在中东技术大学校园案例中实现了高时间分辨率下的高效求解，显著优于全阶段模型和经典Benders方法；通过滚动时域蒙特卡洛模拟验证，所提方法在总成本适度增加下大幅提升了运行可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的建模与求解框架能有效应对多源不确定性，为 campus-scale 能源系统清洁转型提供兼具经济性与鲁棒性的投资运营方案。

Abstract: We study clean energy transition planning for campus-scale integrated electricity-heat systems under both strategic level and operational level uncertainties. We formulate a multi-stage stochastic mixed-integer program that jointly optimizes investment and operational decisions for renewable generation, storage, and heat-transfer technologies whose costs and efficiencies evolve stochastically across stages. To account for short-term operational uncertainty, we further derive a robust reformulation based on box uncertainty sets for demand, renewable generation, and heat-transfer performance. To solve the resulting large-scale model, we develop a Benders decomposition algorithm with partial non-anticipativity relaxation. Investment variables and their non-anticipativity constraints are retained in the master problem while operational variables are assigned to scenario-wise subproblems where their non-anticipativity constraints are relaxed. Non-anticipativity of operational variables is restored only at termination through a smaller linear program. We prove that this correction step can only increase the objective function value by a finite bound. We improve the computational performance of the algorithm developed with valid inequalities and a two-phase cut-addition strategy. Using the proposed approach, we solve the Middle East Technical University campus case study at high temporal resolution within a reasonable computational budget, which is not otherwise possible with the extensive form or the classical Benders decomposition. We evaluate the resulting investment plans through rolling-horizon Monte Carlo simulations with an out-of-sample analysis and demonstrate that the added robustness can significantly improve the operational reliability of the transition plans with a moderate increase in total cost.

</details>


### [101] [Joint Inventory Placement, Assortment Personalization, and Order Fulfillment for Substitutable Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14275)
*Mikhail Fadin,Omar El Housni,Huseyin Topaloglu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线零售商在分布式仓库网络中联合优化库存分配、个性化产品推荐和订单履行的问题，提出了具有理论保证的近似算法，在不同选择模型下实现了渐近最优和常数因子近似比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代在线零售商面临如何在多仓库网络中高效管理库存、个性化推荐并选择最优履约仓库以最大化利润的挑战，现有研究缺乏对三者联合决策的有效解决方案。

Method: 建立了一个包含库存配置、动态个性化选品和履约决策的联合优化模型，客户按离散选择模型选品，企业根据实时库存和客户特征决定履约仓库；针对一般选择模型和MNL模型分别设计了具有理论保证的近似算法。

Result: 在一般选择模型下实现了容量与供应增长时的渐近最优算法；在MNL模型下获得了0.080至0.199之间的常数因子近似算法，是首个对此联合问题提供高效且可证明性能保证的算法工作。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法为分布式仓储系统中的库存、选品与履约联合决策提供了有效的理论支持和实践工具，显著提升了在线零售系统的利润优化能力。

Abstract: Modern online retailers leverage networks of distributed warehouses to rapidly fulfill customer orders. We study a problem of jointly deciding (i) how to allocate inventories across warehouses in the network subject to warehouse capacity and product supply constraints, (ii) how to dynamically select personalized product assortments based on customer preferences and location, as well as real-time stock levels, and (iii) which warehouse to use to fulfill the product chosen by the customer to maximize expected profit. In our model, the firm chooses an inventory placement across warehouses at the start of the selling horizon. Customers of different types then arrive at discrete time periods over a finite time horizon according to a known distribution. When offered an assortment, each customer type chooses at most one product according to a discrete choice model. The firm then chooses a feasible warehouse from which to fulfill the chosen product, depleting its stock and earning a profit that depends on the product, customer type, and warehouse choice. We develop approximation algorithms and policies with provable theoretical guarantees and strong empirical performance. Under general probabilistic choice models, we establish asymptotically optimal approximation algorithms as warehouse capacities and product supplies scale. Under the multinomial logit (MNL) choice model, we obtain constant-factor approximation algorithms, with approximation factors ranging from 0.080 in the most general setting to 0.199 under stationarity and unlimited product supplies, which means that only warehouse capacity constraints are present. To the best of our knowledge, these results provide the first provably efficient algorithms for the joint inventory placement, assortment personalization, and order fulfillment problem.

</details>


### [102] [Diffusion-Based Policies for Dynamic Control of Stochastic Processing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14289)
*Baris Ata,Jin Guang,J. Michael Harrison,Nian Si*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有m个作业类、n个处理活动和p个服务器的加工网络模型，考虑了外生输入流、马尔可夫式作业类转换及线性成本结构。通过建立一个布朗控制问题（BCP）进行扩散近似，并提出可在高维（如m=50）有效求解的数值方法，将解转化为可实施的调度策略。该策略在重载条件下接近最优，并优于以往的低维“等效工作量”方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更一般化地建模复杂的多类队列网络（如允许多种处理方式和可控输入），并解决传统方法在高维下难以计算和解释的问题，作者引入基于布朗控制的近似方法以实现高效且可实施的最优控制。

Method: 建立一个状态空间为m维非负象限的布朗控制问题（BCP），其控制变量是从有界多面体集中根据动态状态选择的漂移向量；利用最新的计算方法进行数值求解，并将结果映射回原始队列系统的控制策略。

Result: 提出的BCP方法可在维度高达m=50时仍有效求解；数值示例表明该策略在重载情形下接近最优，且相比之前低维但难以解释的‘等效工作量’方法更具可解释性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该文提出的基于BCP的控制策略为复杂加工网络提供了高效、可实施且在重载下近优的解决方案，拓展了传统多类队列网络的建模与优化能力。

Abstract: We consider a processing network model with $m$ job classes or buffers, exogenous input flows into some classes, $n$ processing activities, and $p$ servers. Each activity is either a specified server processing jobs of a specified class, or a fictional input server delivering jobs of a specified class; jobs change class in Markovian fashion after completing service. A standard multiclass queueing network, with its one-to-one correspondence between job classes and activities, is a special case, but our general model allows two or more ways to process a given class, and some or all input flows may be turned away at the system manager's discretion.
  Costs are linear: a holding cost per time unit for each class $i$ job in the system, and a rejection penalty for each class $i$ arrival denied access $(i=1,\ldots,m)$. The system manager makes input control, job routing, and order-of-service decisions to minimize expected discounted costs over an infinite horizon.
  We formulate an approximating Brownian control problem (BCP) whose state space is the $m$-dimensional nonnegative orthant; control is a drift vector chosen from a bounded polyhedral set, based on dynamic state observations. Using recently developed computational methods, the BCP can be solved numerically in dimensions up to at least $m=50$, and we explain how the numerical solution is translated into an implementable control policy for the queueing system of original interest.
  Previous work on heavy traffic diffusion approximations suggests that this policy is nearly optimal in the heavy traffic parameter regime, and numerical examples support that conjecture. We also discuss its advantage over an alternative approach, featured in our previous work, where the BCP is replaced by a lower-dimensional "equivalent workload formulation" that is computationally efficient but difficult to interpret in the network of original interest.

</details>


### [103] [Optimal Control with $L^\infty$ and Integral Cost Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14316)
*Madhu Dhiman,Veeraruna Kavitha,Nandyala Hemachandra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了结合累积性能和峰值性能的混合成本控制问题，提出了通过平滑近似方法求解非光滑优化问题的框架，并证明了ε-最优解的存在性及其收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的控制问题通常单独考虑积分成本或L∞成本，但在实际应用中往往需要同时兼顾系统的累积性能和最坏情况性能，因此需要研究两种成本结合的控制问题。

Method: 采用松弛控制框架并利用概率分布表示控制策略，借助紧致性和凸性性质证明最优松弛控制的存在性；引入一族光滑逼近将原问题转化为标准控制问题，并利用最大值定理分析光滑化后问题的解对原问题的逼近效果。

Result: 证明了对于任意ε>0，存在ε-最优的经典控制策略；通过光滑逼近方法得到的纯控制解在平滑参数趋于零时收敛到原问题的ε-最优解。

Conclusion: 本文为具有组合型目标函数（积分+上确界成本）的控制问题提供了理论基础和可行的近似求解方法，验证了在队列管理等实际问题中平衡峰值与累积性能的可行性。

Abstract: Many control problems are classically formulated using integral costs that capture the cumulative performance of a system. Peak or worst-case behavior is captured via supremum or $L^\infty$-costs in another variety of control problems. When both cumulative and peak performance are important, it is natural to consider objective functions that combine the two costs. Although each criterion is well studied, their combination has not been explored extensively and we precisely work on such control problems.
  Towards establishing the existence, we first consider the relaxed framework, where control is considered using probability distributions. Using the well-known compactness and convexity properties of such control spaces, we establish the existence of an optimal relaxed control---we eventually establish the existence of an $ε$-optimal pure (or classical) control, for every $ε> 0.$
  Despite these existence results, computing optimal policies remains challenging due to the non-smoothness introduced by the supremum term, and it is not clear whether the dynamic programming principle holds for our combined problem. To address this, we introduce a family of smooth approximations that yield standard control problems with well-defined optimal (pure) solutions. Using Maximum Theorem, we establish that the solutions of the smooth problems among pure controls form $ε$-optimal for the original problem, with $ε$ tending to zero as the smoothing parameter converges to zero.
  Finally using the methods proposed in this paper, we study a queueing problem to illustrate (among others) that the required trade-off between peak congestion levels and cumulative performance can be achieved.

</details>


### [104] [Optimizing bounds for energy-constrained optimal cooling problems in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14334)
*Pedro Blöss Braga,Giovanni Fantuzzi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了在给定动能预算下二维不可压缩流体的最优冷却控制问题，通过拉格朗日对偶方法建立了具有良好适定性的对偶问题，并利用半正定规划层次结构进行数值求解，同时构造了解析上界，改进了现有冷却效率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在最大化流体温度梯度均方倒数所衡量的冷却效率，在存在冷边界和内部热源/汇的情况下实现能量约束下的最优热管理。

Method: 采用拉格朗日对偶法构建对偶问题，通过离散化得到收敛的半正定规划序列进行数值逼近，并利用区域对称性降低计算复杂度，同时构造对偶问题的可行解以获得解析上界。

Result: 数值求解了方形和环形区域中的最优冷却问题，得到了冷却效率的上界；证明了一般情况下E(Pe) ≲ Pe²，而在圆盘和环形区域且热源具有正向角平均时E(Pe) ≲ Pe²/ln²Pe。

Conclusion: 所提方法为能量受限的冷却流动提供了新的效率上界，推广并改进了已有理论结果，尤其在轴对称情形下获得了更优的渐近估计。

Abstract: We study optimal control problems for incompressible fluids in two-dimensional domains with a cold boundary and internal heat sources and sinks. Given a kinetic energy budget, measured by the square of a nondimensional Péclet number $\mathrm{Pe}$, the goal is to maximize a cooling efficiency inversely proportional to the mean square gradient of the fluid's temperature. Using Lagrange duality, we formulate a well-posed dual problem whose solution yields an upper bound on the maximum cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$. We then numerically approximate the dual problem using a convergent hierarchy of semidefinite programs obtained via discretization. We illustrate this computational approach on optimal cooling problems in a square and in an annulus, explaining also how problem symmetries can be exploited to reduce computational complexity. Finally, we construct admissible points for the dual problem to obtain new analytical upper bounds on the optimal cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$. Specifically, we prove that $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe}) \lesssim \mathrm{Pe}^{2}$ for arbitrary domains and heat distributions, and that $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})\lesssim \mathrm{Pe}^{2}/ \ln^2\mathrm{Pe}$ for cooling flows in disks and annuli with heat source/sink distributions with a positive azimuthal average. These results generalize and improve known efficiency bounds for energy-constrained cooling flows in a disk.

</details>


### [105] [A Shrinkage Path Heuristic for Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14336)
*Lingjun Meng,Ryan Cory-Wright,Wolfram Wiesemann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种收缩路径启发式方法，用于简化Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化（DRO）的求解过程，在降低计算成本的同时保留了大部分性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Wasserstein DRO方法虽然强大，但其确定性重构通常包含难以求解的非凸内子问题，因此需要一种更高效且实用的求解方法。

Method: 通过在样本平均近似（SAA）和经典鲁棒优化解之间的线段上进行一维搜索，构造收缩路径启发式；同时利用对偶形式提供后验次优性界。

Result: 在多商品报童和预约调度问题上的实验表明，该方法能达到Wasserstein DRO相对于SAA 85-110%（或45-70%）的样本外性能提升，且计算成本显著更低。

Conclusion: 收缩路径启发式是一种高效、实用的DRO求解策略，在保持大部分鲁棒性收益的同时大幅降低计算复杂度。

Abstract: Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization (DRO) is a versatile and widely adopted framework for decision-making under uncertainty, yet its standard deterministic reformulations generally contain non-convex inner subproblems that are challenging to solve. To address this issue, we propose a shrinkage path heuristic that reduces the solution of a DRO problem to a one-dimensional search over the line segment connecting the (typically benign) sample average approximation (SAA) and the (more demanding but practically solvable) classical robust optimization solution. We derive a priori suboptimality bounds in stylized settings and, for the general case, a posteriori bounds obtained by applying a similar heuristic to a dual formulation. Numerical experiments on a multi-item newsvendor and an appointment scheduling problem show that the shrinkage path heuristic attains 85-110% (resp. 45-70%) of the out-of-sample performance improvements of Wasserstein DRO over SAA, at a fraction of the computational cost.

</details>


### [106] [AI-Assisted Discovery and Construction of a Counterexample to the Convergence of Three-Block ADMM with the Identity Matrix as its Third Constraint Block](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14396)
*Kenan Xu,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了三块交替方向乘子法（ADMM）在第三约束块为单位矩阵时的收敛性问题，通过Codex与GPT-5.6 Sol构建了一个周期为66的非收敛轨道反例，证明即使前两块为强凸二次函数，直接三块ADMM仍可能发散。同时利用Kimi Code与Kimi K3模型发现周期为23的吸引轨道，并探讨了对偶步长调整对恢复收敛的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决三块ADMM在第三块为单位矩阵时的收敛性未决问题，填补现有理论空白。

Method: 使用Codex结合GPT-5.6 Sol生成有理反例候选，并通过分段仿射约简路径进行精确验证；同时用Kimi Code与Kimi K3模型探索不同路径下的动态行为。

Result: 构造出一个前两块为强凸二次函数、第三块为单位矩阵的三块ADMM不收敛反例，呈现周期66的有界非收敛轨道；发现小的对偶步长可恢复特定实例的收敛，但无统一正相对步长适用于整个类别；Kimi系统独立找到周期23的局部吸引轨道。

Conclusion: 直接三块ADMM即使在特殊结构下也可能不收敛，收敛性依赖于具体问题参数和算法设置，不同研究配置会影响所发现的数学对象与收敛证书。

Abstract: The alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), as a landmark algorithm, has attracted tremendous research attention and extensive practical applications over the past two decades. It is well known that, although the two-block ADMM enjoys well-established theoretical convergence guarantees, its direct extension to the three-block case may fail to converge, as demonstrated by existing counterexamples [5]. However, to the best of our knowledge, the case in which the third constraint block is the identity remains unresolved: the existing literature gives neither a general convergence proof nor a counterexample for this subclass. In this paper, we give a negative answer: direct three-block ADMM may fail even when the first two blocks are strongly convex quadratics. Using Codex with GPT-5.6 Sol, we construct an explicit rational counterexample candidate and verify it along a piecewise-affine reduction path; exact checks show that direct three-block ADMM on this instance produces a bounded nonconvergent orbit of period 66. Within the same Codex workflow, we further guide a study of multiplier relaxation and clarify when convergence can be restored at the fixed-instance and class levels: a problem-dependent small dual step can restore convergence, whereas no positive relative step works uniformly over the whole class. Furthermore, we also test the recent Kimi Code with Kimi K3 model without the Codex candidate or project-specific route guidance; along a different path it produces an exact locally attracting period-23 certificate, convertible to an equivalent all-identity instance. The comparison suggests that different research-harness configurations can shape the mathematical objects explored and the certificates pursued.

</details>


### [107] [Residual-Controlled Douglas--Rachford Splitting for Differentiable Solver Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14470)
*Kang Liu,Jianchen Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为残差控制Douglas-Rachford分裂（RCDRS）的可微分求解器层，用于锥线性规划，通过反馈控制动态调整参数，在保证投影分裂结构的同时提升解的质量、可行性和决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可微分求解器在固定深度展开时难以兼顾解的质量、可行性与计算开销，需要一种能自适应调节的机制来优化整体性能。

Method: 将展开的求解器视为反馈控制的动力系统，引入因果控制器动态调节松弛参数和目标驱动参数，保持Douglas-Rachford分裂的结构，并理论分析其固定点残差界与时变扰动下的收敛性。

Result: 在混合锥基准和工程应用中验证了RCDRS能显著提高解的质量、可行性和下游决策表现，并可从最终分裂状态恢复原对偶诊断信息。

Conclusion: RCDRS通过控制理论与优化算法结合，实现了高效、稳定的可微分求解，在多种任务中优于固定深度展开方法。

Abstract: Differentiable solver layers embed constrained optimization into end-to-end learning systems, but fixed-depth unrolling must trade off solution quality, feasibility, and computational budget. We propose Residual-Controlled Douglas--Rachford Splitting (RCDRS), a differentiable solver layer for conic linear programs. RCDRS treats an unrolled solver as a feedback-controlled dynamical system, where a causal controller adapts the relaxation and objective-drive parameters while preserving the projection-splitting structure of Douglas--Rachford splitting. Theoretically, we show that each fixed admissible block remains an averaged relaxed DRS operator and admits finite-step fixed-point residual bounds. We further analyze safeguarded time-varying rollouts as summable perturbations of a limiting averaged operator, and recover terminal primal-dual diagnostics from the final splitting state. Experiments on mixed-cone benchmarks and engineering applications show that RCDRS improves solution quality, feasibility and downstream decision performance. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RC-DRS-180C/.

</details>


### [108] [On quantitative sufficient second-order optimality conditions for elliptic optimal control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14525)
*Francisco Fuica,Nicolai Jork*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无Tikhonov正则化条件下，受半线性椭圆方程约束的带框约束分布式最优控制问题的二阶最优性定量条件，并提出了保证线性化状态在L²范数下强制性的条件，进而证明了二阶条件在状态和跟踪数据扰动下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对无Tikhonov正则化的半线性椭圆方程约束下的分布式最优控制问题，缺乏稳定的二阶最优性条件，需建立具有鲁棒性的定量判据。

Method: 通过分析二次型形式的二阶变分，提出一个能保证线性化状态在L²范数下具有强制性的定量条件。

Result: 得到了一个可确保二阶变分强制性的定量条件，并证明了该二阶最优性条件在状态与跟踪数据受到扰动时仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 所提出的定量条件有效保证了无正则化情况下最优控制问题二阶条件的稳定性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper, a quantitative condition for optimality for distributed optimal control problems with box-constraints that are subject to a semilinear elliptic equation is considered. An important property of the investigated optimal control problems is the absence of a Tikhonov regularization. It is well known that at a given control, the second variation is a quadratic form in the linearized state, and its curvature coefficient function may vanish or change sign. We present a quantitative condition that implies coercivity with respect to the $L^2$-norm of the linearized-states. As a consequence, the stability of the second-order condition under perturbations of the states and the tracking data is shown.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Consistent Model Chasing Is Minimax Optimal: The Exact Value of Scalar Adversarial Adaptive Control under Large Parametric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13651)
*Dimitar Ho*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文解决了在对抗性扰动下具有参数不确定性的标量系统的自适应控制中的一个基本问题，首次给出了最坏情况下峰值性能的精确值，并证明了基于一致模型追逐的确定性等效死区控制是最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论无法提供具有任意大小参数不确定性自适应控制问题在最坏情况峰值性能下的精确值，仅能给出稳定性保证、增益界或遗憾率。本文旨在填补这一空白，求解该类问题的精确最优性能指标。

Method: 通过分析标量系统 $x_{t+1} = ax_t + u_t + w_t$ 在未知参数 $a$ 和有界扰动 $w_t$ 下的行为，构建因果控制器，并利用集合成员一致性区间上的确定性等效死区控制策略；结合博弈论视角，推导出最坏情况下的最小峰值性能值，并证明该策略的强制最优性。

Result: 得到了该对抗性自适应控制问题的精确最坏情况峰值性能值 $\gamma^\star(\Delta) = 1 + \Delta$，其中1为扰动带来的不可减少代价，$\Delta$为识别过程中不可避免的单次峰值代价；最优策略是在集合成员一致性区间的中点执行确定性等效死区控制，并证明该结构是强制最优而非仅充分。

Conclusion: 本文首次为对抗性环境下的自适应控制提供了精确的最优性证明，揭示了无需主动探索的一致模型追逐策略即可实现最优性能，挑战了传统需要激励或乐观主义的方法，为自适应控制设计提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: We solve exactly a fundamental problem of adaptive control against adversarial disturbances: regulate the scalar system $x_{t+1} = ax_t + u_t + w_t$, $x_0=0$, $\|w\|_\infty \le 1$, where the constant pole $a \in [-Δ, Δ]$ is unknown in sign and magnitude and $Δ$ is arbitrarily large. Elementary as the system looks, the least worst-case peak $\|x\|_\infty$ that a causal controller can guarantee against an adversarial pair $(a, w)$ (the value of this game) has, to our knowledge, never been determined for any adaptive control problem with parametric uncertainty of arbitrary size under this criterion; existing theory supplies stability certificates, gain bounds, and regret rates, not the value. That value is $γ^\star(Δ) = 1 + Δ$ for every $Δ>0$. The summand $1$ is the irreducible price of the disturbance, and $Δ$ the exact price of a single, unavoidable identification spike. The optimal policy is certainty-equivalent deadbeat control at the midpoint of the set-membership consistent interval, an instance of the robust oracle $\times$ consistent model chasing architecture. The architecture is forced, not merely sufficient: writing $θ_t := -u_t/x_t$ exhibits every causal controller as an oracle-selector composition, and optimality pins the selector to the midpoint at the critical histories. The standard tools, classical and modern, each fail quantifiably: probing is punished before it pays, commitment is fatal at sub-disturbance excitation once adaptation is necessary, optimism degenerates to tie-breaking or pays asymptotically at least twice the optimum, and regret certificates are blind to the worst-case peak in both directions. The optimal law contains no exploration mechanism, its learning purely passive. These results give the first exact optimality certificate for consistent model chasing as a design principle for adversarial adaptive control.

</details>


### [110] [Control Barrier--Value Functions under Partial Observability: Safety Guarantees via Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13819)
*Niloofar Jahanshahi,Mo Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对部分可观测非线性控制系统的安全分析与控制器综合框架，通过结合哈密顿-雅可比可达性与控制屏障函数，并利用共形预测处理估计误差，实现了在状态信息不完整情况下的概率安全性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测系统中，传统基于全状态信息的安全分析方法不适用，因此需要发展能够处理状态估计误差并提供严格安全保证的新方法。

Method: 扩展了控制屏障-值函数（CBVF）框架，引入共形预测获取估计误差的边界，并将其融入估计空间的安全性分析，构造适用于部分可观测系统的CBVF安全证书，进而设计基于QP的在线安全滤波器。

Result: 推导出真实系统状态的有限时间域内概率安全保证，并在部分可观测避障案例中验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在部分可观测条件下有效实现非线性系统的实时安全控制，为存在有界扰动的仿射系统提供了可行的安全滤波方案。

Abstract: This paper studies safety analysis and controller synthesis for partially observable nonlinear control systems. We extend the control barrier--value function (CBVF) framework, which combines Hamilton--Jacobi reachability and control barrier functions, to settings where full state information is not available and control is based on an estimated state. Given an estimator, we apply conformal prediction to the estimation error and obtain an error bound at a user-chosen miscoverage level. We incorporate this bound into the estimator-space safety analysis and define a CBVF-based safety certificate for partially observable systems. We then derive a finite-horizon probabilistic safety guarantee for the true system state. Finally, we propose a QP-based online safety filter for systems affine in the control and disturbance, whose solution enforces the CBVF safety condition in real time against bounded disturbance. The proposed framework is illustrated on a partially observable obstacle-avoidance case study.

</details>


### [111] [Hierarchical Sensor-Spoofing Defence Framework for Networked DC Microgrids via Cyber-Physical Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13823)
*Mengxiang Liu,Xin Zhang,Shiyi Zhao,Ruilong Deng*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多层信息物理协同的分层框架，用于检测和缓解网络化微电网中的传感器欺骗攻击，通过在关键点部署物理防护并结合主动检测与协调缓解策略，在实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对传感器欺骗攻击的防御方法在单个分布式能源系统中各有优劣，但在多个分布式能源系统间的多层协同防御研究尚不充分，尤其缺乏对网络化微电网中跨层高效协同的系统性研究。

Method: 提出一种分层防御框架，仅在微电网的公共耦合点（PCC）部署物理防护技术，保障关键传感器数据安全，并在此基础上采用基于微电网-分布式能源协调的主动检测机制，通过参数扰动暴露智能欺骗攻击，随后启动递归估计方法协同校正传感器偏差。

Result: 该框架能有效识别多种传感器欺骗攻击，准确估计并补偿传感器偏差，实验结果表明其在不同攻击场景下均具有良好的检测与恢复能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的多层信息物理协同框架能够在控制成本的同时提升网络化微电网的安全性，为分布式能源系统的抗攻击设计提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: In parallel to the cyber attack that manipulates the reference points of distributed energy resources (DERs) by maliciously accessing the remote monitoring and control system, the vulnerability of voltage/current sensors to electromagnetic interference (EMI) in the physical domain has been widely discussed. Existing research efforts against sensor spoofing attacks can be classified into physical prevention and cyber detection/mitigation. These defence methods each have strengths and weaknesses in balancing cost, security, and performance in a single DER, yet systematic research on their multi-layer efficient coordination across DERs remains limited. Towards this end, this paper proposes a hierarchical framework to detect and mitigate sensor spoofing attacks in networked microgrids (NMGs) via {multi-layer cyber-physical coordination}. It requires only to deploy physical prevention technologies at critical points, i.e., the local points of common coupling (PCC) of MGs, such that cyber detection/mitigation algorithms can be adopted based on the secured sensor readings to counter sensor spoofing attacks in DERs. The framework employs an MG-DER coordinated proactive detection scheme to strategically trigger parameter perturbations, under which the intelligent sensor spoofing attacks can be {effectively} disclosed. Afterwards, mitigation schemes based on MG-DER coordination are activated to recursively and accurately estimate sensor biases. Experiments on a cyber-physical DC NMG testbed confirm the framework's effectiveness across diverse attack scenarios.

</details>


### [112] [Real-Time In-Domain Congestion Control for the LWR Traffic Model via Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13841)
*Zehang Zhu,Brian Block,Stephanie Stockar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出基于控制屏障函数的实时交通拥堵控制方法，将交通拥堵问题转化为分布式安全控制问题，并通过离散化和KKT分析转化为高维二次规划问题，设计了具有收敛性保证的结构化原对偶活动集算法，实现了实时安全控制输入的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决Lighthill-Whitham-Richards交通模型中的实时区域内交通拥堵控制问题，需要一种能够确保交通安全并实现实时计算的控制方法。

Method: 采用控制屏障函数方法，将交通拥堵建模为分布式安全控制问题；通过离散化和KKT条件分析，将其转化为高维二次规划问题；设计了一种结构利用型原对偶活动集算法来求解该优化问题，以实现实时安全控制输入的计算。

Result: 所提出的算法能够在保证收敛的前提下实时计算出安全控制输入，数值仿真表明该方法在不同名义控制器下均具有良好的有效性和实时可行性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法能有效处理连续交通流模型中的实时安全控制问题，具备实际应用潜力，尤其适用于需要快速响应的交通管理系统。

Abstract: This paper presents a control barrier function-based method for real-time in-domain congestion control of the Lighthill-Whitham-Richards traffic model. Traffic congestion is formulated as a distributed safety control problem, leading to a infinite-dimensional optimization problem. Through the discretization and Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) analysis, the problem is converted into a high-dimensional quadratic program. A structure-exploiting primal-dual active set algorithm is then developed to compute the safe control input in real time, with convergence guarantees. Numerical simulations with different nominal controllers demonstrate the effectiveness and real-time feasibility of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [113] [Feedback-Guided DNN-Based Controller Fusion for Robust Fixed-Parameter Active Noise Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14061)
*Lu Bai,Yiming He,Xiaofeng Nan,Kai Chen,Jing Lu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于反馈引导的DNN控制器融合框架，用于鲁棒的固定参数主动噪声控制，结合因果WaveNet控制器与混合专家系统，提升对变化声学条件的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决自适应主动噪声控制方法在实际部署中可能不稳定或发散的问题，并克服固定参数控制器在噪声特性变化时性能下降的局限。

Method: 结合因果WaveNet控制器与反馈引导的混合专家（MoE）模块，利用门控网络根据当前声学条件动态加权多个预训练FIR专家模型。

Result: 在耳机主动噪声控制实验中，实现了显著的低频噪声衰减，在1-8 kHz范围内噪声放大可忽略，且支持逐样本流式推理，计算负载均匀分布。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不进行在线参数更新的情况下提升了对变化声学环境的鲁棒性，适用于高效、稳定的实时ANC系统。

Abstract: In active noise control (ANC) systems, adaptive approaches may suffer from instability or divergence, limiting their practical deployment. Consequently, fixed-parameter controllers are widely adopted, but their performance degrades under varying noise characteristics and acoustic path conditions. This paper proposes a feedback-guided DNN-based controller fusion framework for robust fixed-parameter ANC. The proposed method combines a causal WaveNet controller with a feedback-guided mixture-of-experts (MoE) module, where a gating network estimates the weights of multiple pre-trained FIR experts according to the current acoustic condition. The proposed approach improves robustness to varying acoustic conditions without online parameter updating. Furthermore, the model is fully causal and supports sample-wise streaming inference, with computational costs evenly distributed across sampling points to reduce peak computational load. Experimental results on headphone ANC demonstrate substantial low-frequency noise reduction with negligible noise amplification over 1-8 kHz.

</details>


### [114] [A Temporal Barrier Framework for Collision Avoidance in Multi-Agent Autonomous Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14239)
*Benedikt Barthel Sorensen,Mitchell Black,Erfaun Noorani,Themistoklis Sapsis*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为对抗性时间到碰撞（aTTC）的风险度量，并将其嵌入控制屏障函数（CBF）框架中，实现在动态不确定和潜在对抗环境中自主飞行器的安全控制，仿真结果显示其在航点进展和碰撞率方面显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态、不确定且可能具有对抗性的环境中，自主飞行器需要可靠而选择性强的安全协议，以实现近距离飞行同时推进任务目标。

Method: 引入对抗性时间到碰撞（aTTC）作为风险度量，将屏障直接定义在时间域而非距离或速度域，并结合可微分神经网络代理实现实时计算，嵌入CBF框架中形成aTTC-CBF方法。

Result: 在3D独立追踪和编队飞行的长时程仿真中，aTTC-CBF相比高阶基于距离的CBF基线方法，在碰撞率减半的同时实现了高达两倍的航点进展。

Conclusion: aTTC-CBF具有内在的前瞻性，能有效平衡安全性与任务效率，适用于复杂动态环境中的多智能体安全控制。

Abstract: Operating teams of autonomous aircraft in dynamic, uncertain, and potentially adversarial environments requires safety protocols that are reliable yet selective, and allow agents to fly in close proximity while making progress toward mission objectives. We introduce adversarial time-to-collision (aTTC), a risk metric that quantifies, for a given agent, how quickly any surrounding agent could reach it assuming adversarial intent. We embed aTTC into the control barrier function (CBF) framework, defining the barrier directly in time rather than distance or velocity. The resulting aTTC-CBF is inherently anticipatory: agents modulate their own velocity based not on whether a peer is on a collision course, but on how quickly one could reach collision given its dynamical constraints. A differentiable neural-network surrogate makes the aTTC computable in real time within a standard CBF quadratic program. Across long time-horizon simulations of 3D independent-pursuit and formation-flight scenarios, the aTTC-CBF achieves up to twice the waypoint progress at half the collision rate of a higher-order distance-based CBF baseline.

</details>


### [115] [Diagonalizable Directed Laplacians by Positive Arc-Weight Design for Master Stability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14439)
*Aandrew Baggio Sahaya Arokiadoss*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对有向图网络拉普拉斯算子不可对角化问题的加权方法，通过构造弱连通生成有向无环图并合理赋予权重，使得加权后的拉普拉斯矩阵可对角化，从而恢复主稳定性函数变分方程的解耦形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主稳定性函数依赖于可对角化的网络拉普拉斯矩阵，但有向图的拉普拉斯矩阵可能不可对角化，限制了该方法的应用。本文旨在解决这一限制，扩展MSF在有向网络中的适用性。

Method: 首先提取原图的一个弱连通生成有向无环图，确保每个根强连通分量中恰好有一个源点；然后为顶点分配互不相同的加权入度，并将剩余边赋予一个足够小的公共正权重，利用判别式准则确定该权重的可行区间。

Result: 证明了每个弱连通有向图都存在一种严格正的弧权重分配方式，使得其加权入度拉普拉斯矩阵可对角化，非零特征值互异，零特征值半单且重数等于根强连通分量的数量。

Conclusion: 该方法成功使有向网络的拉普拉斯矩阵可对角化，恢复了主稳定性函数中变分方程的完全解耦形式，拓展了MSF在复杂有向网络中的应用基础。

Abstract: The standard master stability function (MSF) formulation has traditionally relied on a diagonalizable network Laplacian, since diagonalizability allows the variational equations to be decomposed into independent equations. Directed Laplacians, however, need not be diagonalizable. We show that every weakly connected digraph admits a strictly positive arc weighting for which its weighted in-degree Laplacian is diagonalizable. Our construction first extracts a weakly connected spanning directed acyclic graph having exactly one source vertex in each root strongly connected component and assigns positive weights so that the weighted indegrees of all remaining vertices are pairwise distinct. The remaining arcs of the original digraph are then assigned a common sufficiently small positive weight. The nonzero eigenvalues remain pairwise distinct under this perturbation, while the zero eigenvalue is semisimple, with multiplicity equal to the number of root strongly connected components. Consequently, the resulting Laplacian admits a complete set of eigenvectors and restores the fully decoupled form of the MSF variational equations. We further give a discriminant-based criterion for computing an admissible interval of the common arc weight.

</details>


### [116] [Optimal Scheduling of Road Maintenance Jobs Considering Impact on Traffic Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14491)
*Charitha Nandepu,Lohitha Kalepu,Gabriele Ciavarella,SangWoo Park*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的代理模型，用于近似网络维护规划中的均衡路段流量，避免重复求解计算成本高昂的交通分配问题，并通过新泽西纽瓦克地区的实际案例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在道路养护规划中需要反复求解因道路容量减少引起的均衡交通流，传统均衡分配模型计算成本高，难以嵌入调度优化框架。

Method: 利用基于优化的均衡解作为真实标签，构建数据驱动的代理模型，直接从起讫点需求预测均衡路段流量。

Result: 在纽瓦克地区的实际数据上验证了该方法的有效性，能够高效逼近精确的均衡流量，具备良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的代理模型可作为未来道路养护调度框架中可扩展的构建模块，显著降低计算负担。

Abstract: Network-level maintenance planning requires repeated evaluations of equilibrium traffic flows under road capacity reductions. While equilibrium traffic assignment models are well established, their repeated solution quickly becomes computationally prohibitive and challenging to embed within maintenance scheduling problems. This paper investigates data-driven surrogate models that approximate equilibrium arc flows directly from origin-destination demand, using optimization-based equilibrium solutions as ground truth. A real-world case study based on traffic data from the Newark, New Jersey area demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed approach as a scalable building block for future maintenance scheduling frameworks.

</details>

### [117] [From the Earth to the Sun](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13635)
*Dieynaba Young*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文讲述了法国科学院成立360周年之际，回顾其17世纪大胆开展测量日地距离的远征历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纪念法国科学院成立360周年，并重现其早期在科学探索中的重要贡献。

Method: 通过历史文献回顾与资料整理，叙述17世纪科学远征的过程，并结合现代数据更新信息。

Result: 还原了法国科学院早期测量日地距离的科学探险历程，补充了原始稿件未包含的内容和最新参考资料。

Conclusion: 该远征体现了早期科学家的勇气与求知精神，为现代天文学发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: This year marks 360 years since the founding of the French Academy of Sciences. This is the story of the academy's daring 17th century expedition to measure the Earth-sun distance. A truncated version first appeared in National Geographic Magazine; the following is closer to the original draft, with updated information and references.

</details>


### [118] [Scientific understanding across disciplines: in varietate concordia?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14378)
*Henk W. de Regt,Milo D. Cornelissen,Vincent Coumans,Nina S. de Boer,Sebastian De Haro,Paul H. W. Disberg,Kristian González Barman,Maarten G. Kleinhans,Daniel Kostić,Ludo L. J. Schoenmakers*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文应用科学理解的语境理论，比较分析了九个科学学科中获得理解的方法和标准的学科差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示不同科学学科在实现理解方面的方法与标准上的异同。

Method: 基于De Regt（2017）提出的科学理解的语境理论，对数学、理论物理、天体物理、化学、地球科学、生物学、神经科学、心理学和工程科学这九个学科进行描述性分析，并进行跨学科比较。

Result: 发现了各学科在追求理解时存在显著的语境依赖性，同时识别出一些共同特征；语境理论能够有效解释这些差异与共性。

Conclusion: 科学理解具有明显的学科特异性，但语境理论为跨学科的理解研究提供了有力框架。

Abstract: Understanding is a generally acknowledged aim of science, but the way in which understanding is pursued and criteria for when it is achieved differ widely across scientific disciplines. This paper offers a comparative analysis of this disciplinary variation, aimed at revealing differences as well as commonalities in methods and criteria for achieving understanding. Starting point is the contextual theory of scientific understanding (De Regt 2017), which was developed to accommodate contextual variation in criteria for understanding. While this work focused on historical variation and used mainly case studies from the history of physics, the current paper applies the contextual theory to disciplinary variation. We present brief descriptive accounts of how understanding figures in nine scientific disciplines: mathematics, theoretical physics, astrophysics, chemistry, geoscience, biology, neuroscience, psychology, and the engineering sciences. Subsequently, we compare and evaluate these accounts in light of the contextual theory of scientific understanding.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Learning Spin Hamiltonians from Terahertz Two-Dimensional Coherent Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14460)
*Martin Mootz,Chuankun Huang,Liang Luo,Jigang Wang,Yong-Xin Yao*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于非线性太赫兹二维相干光谱的监督机器学习框架，用于从实验数据中推断量子材料的有效哈密顿量参数，并在稀土正铁氧体中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定量子材料中有效哈密顿量的参数是一个具有挑战性的逆问题，传统方法难以直接从实验中提取微观相互作用参数。

Method: 构建一个监督机器学习框架：首先使用校准的正向模型生成候选哈密顿量对应的光谱，通过统一的预处理流程将模拟和实验光谱映射到相同表示空间，再训练神经网络学习从光谱特征到微观参数的逆映射关系。

Result: 在Sm$_{0.4}$Er$_{0.6}$FeO$_3$的实验THz-2DCS数据上成功推断出物理合理的哈密顿量参数；合成基准测试表明非线性光谱包含超出线性响应范围的参数信息，且多脉冲延迟可提升抗噪能力与推断精度。

Conclusion: 非线性太赫兹二维相干光谱结合机器学习为有效哈密顿量的参数识别和模型优化提供了有力工具，有助于实现对量子材料微观相互作用的实验驱动式理解、预测与调控。

Abstract: Effective Hamiltonians connect microscopic interactions to measurable collective behavior in quantum materials, but determining their parameters directly from experiment remains a challenging inverse problem. We introduce a supervised machine-learning framework that infers Hamiltonian parameters from nonlinear terahertz two-dimensional coherent spectra. A calibrated forward model generates spectra from candidate Hamiltonians, a common preprocessing pipeline maps simulated and experimental spectra into the same representation, and a neural network learns the inverse map from spectral fingerprints to microscopic parameters. We demonstrate the approach for rare-earth orthoferrites using a two-sublattice Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert spin model with exchange, Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction, anisotropies, and damping. Synthetic benchmarks show that nonlinear spectra encode parameters beyond those fixed by the linear response, with inference accuracy tracking the physical spectral sensitivity and robustness against noise improved by using multiple inter-pulse delays. Applied to experimental THz-2DCS data from Sm$_{0.4}$Er$_{0.6}$FeO$_3$, the inferred parameters yield physically reasonable forward simulations, while remaining discrepancies identify limitations of the reduced model. These results establish THz-2DCS as a data-rich platform for effective-Hamiltonian inference and model refinement, enabling experimentally driven identification of microscopic interactions while providing a foundation for understanding, predicting, and ultimately controlling the emergent properties of quantum materials.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [On first-order thermodynamic equilibrium conditions for fluid-fluid and solid-phase interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13603)
*Nicodemo Di Pasquale,Thomas Hudson*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的热力学框架，通过变分原理同时描述体相和界面平衡条件，适用于具有显著表面效应的固-液和液-液系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期理论忽略了界面对系统的影响，无法准确描述表面效应显著的多相系统，因此需要一种能同时处理体相和界面热力学的统一框架。

Method: 基于Larché和Cahn的约束变分公式，将界面热力学直接纳入变分原理，首先在流体-流体系统中建立数学结构，再推广至固体-流体界面。

Result: 体相和界面平衡条件作为单一热力学泛函的驻立条件自然出现，经典非均质系统的平衡关系通过不同类型的允许变分得出。

Conclusion: 该框架为体相、界面和构型热力学提供了统一的理论基础，能够更完整地描述涉及显著表面效应的多相系统。

Abstract: Starting from the constrained variational formulation of Larché and Cahn for solids in contact with fluids, a unified thermodynamic framework for such systems is developed. Both bulk and interfacial equilibrium conditions arise as stationarity conditions of a single thermodynamic functional. While earlier theory neglects a full treatment of interfacial contributions and therefore cannot describe systems in which surface effects are significant, the present formulation incorporates interfacial thermodynamics directly into the variational principle. The framework is introduced first for fluid-fluid systems to establish the underlying mathematical structure, and is then extended to solid-fluid interfaces. Within this setting, classical equilibrium relations for heterogeneous systems emerge naturally from different classes of admissible variations, providing a common theoretical basis for bulk, interfacial, and configurational thermodynamics.

</details>


### [121] [Far-from-equilibrium topological phase transition in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13658)
*Ze-Min Huang,Gustav John,Sebastian Diehl*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种远离平衡态的拓扑相变机制，基于一维紧致相位模型在随机初始条件下确定性演化，通过拓扑与对称性而非唯象假设揭示了涡旋与均匀固定点之间的竞争导致连续非平衡相变，并确认其普适类为有向渗流（DP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究远离平衡态下的拓扑相变机制，探索不依赖唯象假设、而由拓扑和对称性驱动的相变原理。

Method: 利用一维紧致相位模型，在随机初始条件下进行确定性演化，构建相应的有效场论，并通过动力学标度分析进行数值验证。

Result: 发现涡旋诱导的无序化与趋向均匀性的弛豫之间的竞争导致连续非平衡相变，确认该相变属于有向渗流（DP）普适类。

Conclusion: 拓扑与对称性足以驱动远离平衡态的拓扑相变，且其普适性可归类为有向渗流，为非平衡相变提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We uncover a mechanism for far-from-equilibrium topological phase transitions, via a one-dimensional compact phase model evolving deterministically from random initial conditions. It rests on topology and symmetry rather than on phenomenological postulates: phase compactness permits vortices, and a homogeneous fixed point suppresses their nucleation, with the fixed point itself implied by phase-shift symmetry. The competition between vortex-induced disordering and relaxation toward homogeneity drives a continuous nonequilibrium transition, whose universality class we identify as directed percolation (DP). We demonstrate this by constructing the corresponding effective field theory and numerically confirming DP critical scaling through dynamical-scaling analysis.

</details>


### [122] [Far-from-equilibrium scaling of non-abelian Goldstone modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13666)
*Carl Philipp Zelle,Gustav John,Orla Supple,Romain Daviet,Sebastian Diehl*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续对称性破缺与弱非平衡驱动相互作用下产生的一类广泛的非热相，扩展了Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）物理，构建了描述具有共存时间与内禀序的非阿贝尔时间晶体的非平衡非线性σ模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在驱动量子材料、活性物质和光诱导周期态中自然出现的SO(2)×O(N)对称结构下的非平衡非热相及其动力学行为。

Method: 构建了适用于SO(2)×O(N)对称性的非平衡非线性σ模型，推导了旋转和振荡相的戈德斯通理论，并通过单圈重整化群分析研究其标度行为。

Result: 发现d=1,2时弱非平衡扰动会破坏平衡固定点并生成强耦合非热固定点；d>2时有效平衡恢复；旋转相中不同戈德斯通模式获得不同的普适动力学指数，表现出非常规弱动态标度；并通过1+1维直接模拟验证了该标度行为。

Conclusion: 将KPZ普适类推广到非阿贝尔对称性破缺体系，揭示了非热标度窗口的存在，为实验上观测非热相提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We identify a broad class of nonthermal phases generated by the interplay of continuous symmetry breaking and weak nonequilibrium driving. Extending Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) physics beyond the single $SO(2)$ chronon associated with periodically broken time translations, we construct nonequilibrium nonlinear sigma models for $SO(2)\times O(N)$ symmetry, describing non-Abelian time crystals with coexisting temporal and internal order. This symmetry structure arises naturally in driven quantum materials, active matter, and optically induced periodic states. For rotating and oscillating phases, we derive the Goldstone theories and show that chronon-$O(N)$ couplings remain finite deep in the ordered regime. One-loop renormalization group analysis reveals a KPZ-like dimensional structure: in $d=1,2$, arbitrarily weak nonequilibrium perturbations destabilize the equilibrium fixed point and generate strongly coupled nonthermal fixed points, realizing emergent equilibrium breaking. By contrast, for $d > 2$, weak perturbations are irrelevant and effective equilibrium is restored. A central result is unconventional weak dynamic scaling in the rotating phase: strongly coupled Goldstone sectors acquire distinct universal dynamical exponents despite belonging to the same order parameter. We characterize this scaling analytically and corroborate it through direct simulations in $1+1$ dimensions. In the oscillating phase, we recover and extend weak-scaling regimes known from drifting polymers. Finally, compactness and topological defects ultimately destroy long-range order but leave experimentally accessible nonthermal scaling windows. Together, these results extend KPZ universality to non-Abelian symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [123] [Small-world structure of quantum computer hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13855)
*S. J. da Silva Junior,D. L. Shepelyansky,J. Lages*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了量子计算机中量子寄存器态网络的小世界特性，发现其类似于社会网络中的“六度分隔”现象，并通过Āberg准则构建有效网络，揭示了逾渗相变与量子混沌及热化 onset 的紧密关联，且方法可扩展至30个量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子系统中残余两体相互作用导致的复杂网络特性，以及这些特性如何影响量子混沌和热化行为。

Method: 使用Āberg准则（仅保留超过局域能级间距的相互作用）构建有效网络模型，并分析其小世界特性和逾渗相变行为。

Result: 发现量子寄存器态网络具有小世界特性，最可能的Ārdős数约为9；当耦合强度超过临界值时，有效网络形成贯穿整个量子寄存器空间的巨大连通分支；这一逾渗转变与量子混沌和动力学热化的 onset 高度一致。

Conclusion: 量子计算机中的残余相互作用可导致类似复杂社会网络的小世界效应和逾渗相变，这为理解量子混沌和热化提供了新的高效分析框架，并可推广到多达30个量子比特的系统。

Abstract: We show that the network of quantum register states of a quantum computer (QC), coupled through residual two-body interactions between qubits, exhibits small-world properties analogous to those of complex networks found in human society. The most probable Erdős number between any two states is about 9, comparable to the six degrees of separation reported by Milgram for social networks. Using the $\mathring{A}$berg criterion, which retains only interactions exceeding the local energy spacing, we construct an effective ($\mathring{A}$berg) network and show that, above a critical coupling strength, this network percolates into a giant component spanning nearly the whole quantum register space. This percolation transition closely matches the onset of quantum chaos and dynamical thermalization established previously via costly exact diagonalization, while our approach extends the accessible system size up to $n_q=30$ qubits.

</details>


### [124] [Generalizing the multidimensional thermodynamic uncertainty relation to combinations of arbitrary counting variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14276)
*Niklas Buschmann,Udo Seifert,Alexander M. Maier*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多维热力学不确定性关系（MTUR）的广义形式，适用于时间依赖驱动过程，并可处理多种类型的波动计数观测量及其粗粒化观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更准确地估计部分可访问马尔可夫网络中的隐藏量（如平均熵产生率和总动力学活性），需要推广现有的不确定性关系。

Method: 通过利用一组观测净电流的协方差，将多维TUR推广到具有任意初始状态和任意测量时长的时间依赖驱动过程中，并扩展至包含时间反对称、时间对称或时间非对称的波动计数观测量。

Result: 得到了一个统一的广义不确定性关系，能够 tighter 地约束隐藏量，并适用于多种类型观测量及粗粒化观测情形。

Conclusion: 所提出的广义MTUR为分析非平衡系统提供了更强有力的工具，涵盖了现有TUR及其扩展形式的主要特性。

Abstract: Uncertainty relations provide lower bounds for otherwise hidden quantities of a partially accessible Markov network like the mean entropy production rate and the total dynamical activity. The thermodynamic uncertainty relation (TUR) is arguably the most prominent one and involves the precision of a fluctuating net current. One of its major generalizations is the multidimensional TUR (MTUR), which yields a tighter bound by using covariances of a set of observed net currents. We generalize this latter bound to time-dependently driven processes with arbitrary initial state and arbitrary measurement duration. Furthermore, this bound can be used with a set of fluctuating counting observables each of which can be time-antisymmetric, time-symmetric or time-asymmetric, i.e., a net current, a traffic or a flow. We can even allow for coarse-grained observations in which each counting observable could consist of multiple indiscernable observed transitions of the underlying system. Thus, we generalize the MTUR, extensions of the TUR for time-dependent processes based on one current, and an estimator based on one flux or on one traffic in a unifying way. We illustrate this general uncertainty relation with simple examples.

</details>


### [125] [Linear response across interaction regimes in two-dimensional ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14477)
*Aaron Müller,Pavel E. Dolgirev,Oleksii Malyshev,Eugene Demler*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于高斯函数表示分布函数的新方法，有效求解二维铁磁体中的线性量子玻尔兹曼方程，揭示了从弹道到水动力学输运的温度驱动 crossover，并在单层 CrCl₃ 中与实验结果取得一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维铁磁体中自旋输运性质需要同时描述低动量和高动量磁振子的不同相互作用行为，传统量子玻尔兹曼方程因碰撞积分复杂而难以求解。

Method: 发展了一种将分布函数表示为高斯函数之和的高效方法，使多维碰撞积分变得可处理，从而实现线性量子玻尔兹曼方程的精确求解，并计算动量和频率分辨的线性响应。

Result: 成功揭示了二维铁磁体中随温度变化从弹道输运到集体水动力学输运的 crossover；在单层 CrCl₃ 中的计算结果与氮-空位中心去相位实验观测到的异常磁噪声一致。

Conclusion: 该方法为研究基于量子玻尔兹曼动力学的二维量子系统中的动量和频率分辨线性响应提供了一个通用且有效的框架。

Abstract: Recent discoveries of two-dimensional (2D) ferromagnets have stimulated intense interest in understanding and controlling their spin transport properties. A central microscopic feature of these systems is that exchange-driven magnon--magnon interactions are strongly momentum dependent: low-momentum magnons interact weakly, while high-momentum ones can scatter strongly and exhibit collective hydrodynamic behavior. Understanding transport in such systems therefore requires a microscopic description capable of capturing ballistic and hydrodynamic regimes on equal footing. The natural framework is the quantum Boltzmann equation (QBE), whose solution is notoriously difficult because of the multidimensional collision integrals. Here, we develop a method based on an efficient representation of distribution functions as sums of Gaussians, which renders the collision integrals tractable. This approach enables accurate solution of the linearized QBE and computation of momentum- and frequency-resolved linear response in 2D ferromagnets across a broad range of temperatures and magnetic fields. In particular, we resolve a temperature-driven crossover from a ballistic regime dominated by weakly interacting low-momentum magnons to a collective hydrodynamic regime governed by strongly interacting high-momentum modes. Applying this method to monolayer CrCl$_3$, we obtain good agreement with recent nitrogen-vacancy-center dephasing experiments that reported anomalous magnetic noise consistent with magnon sound. More broadly, our work establishes a general framework for computing momentum- and frequency-resolved linear response in interacting 2D quantum systems describable within quantum Boltzmann kinetics.

</details>
